# Functions

Every function defined outside a class, A to Z. Class methods are on their [class pages](/code/classes/index.md).

## A

| Function | File | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| [activeTenantFilter()](/code/functions/activeTenantFilter.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | Generic active-company list filter for any tenant-scoped table (Change Management onward). Same semantics as ticketTenantFilter but the table alias and column are parameterised so each Phase-3 module can reuse it: scopes to the analyst's ACTIVE company, and the Default company also owns NULL-tenant rows (so nothing is hidden at N=1 or from Default). Returns ['', []] at N=1. |
| [activeTenantReadFilter()](/code/functions/activeTenantReadFilter.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | `activeTenantFilter()` for a LIST READ that should widen in the "All companies" view — the opt-in the note above `isActiveTenantAll()` describes. |
| [addCheck()](/code/functions/addCheck.md) | [api/messaging/slack_diagnose.php](/code/files/api/messaging/slack_diagnose.php.md) | Appends one result row (key, label, status, detail and an optional fix) to the global `$checks` list the diagnosis returns. |
| [addSection()](/code/functions/addSection.md) | [api/system/debug-tools/D001_demo_core_import.php](/code/files/api/system/debug-tools/D001_demo_core_import.php.md) | Adds one titled section ("=== TITLE ===" and its lines) to the report; `$body` is a string or an array of lines. |
| [aiAzureApiVersionLooksValid()](/code/functions/aiAzureApiVersionLooksValid.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | Whether an API version looks like one Azure publishes. |
| [aiAzureChatUrl()](/code/functions/aiAzureChatUrl.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | Build the URL for an Azure OpenAI deployment-based chat call. |
| [aiAzureIsV1()](/code/functions/aiAzureIsV1.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | An API version of "v1" means Azure's newer route, which has no api-version. |
| [aiExplainFailure()](/code/functions/aiExplainFailure.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | What a failed call most likely means, in a sentence an administrator can act on - or '' when the error says it plainly enough already. |
| [aiOpenAICompatPost()](/code/functions/aiOpenAICompatPost.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | POST an OpenAI-format chat request, putting right the two parameters newer models refuse by name. |
| [aiProviderCallAnthropic()](/code/functions/aiProviderCallAnthropic.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | Sends one system + user prompt to the Anthropic Messages API and returns the joined text blocks with token counts and the stop reason. |
| [aiProviderCallOpenAICompatible()](/code/functions/aiProviderCallOpenAICompatible.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | The OpenAI chat-completions wire format, at whatever URL and behind whatever auth header the caller names. |
| [aiProviderChat()](/code/functions/aiProviderChat.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | Send a one-shot chat request and return a normalised result. |
| [aiProviderChatTools()](/code/functions/aiProviderChatTools.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | ─── Tool calling ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── |
| [aiProviderHttpPost()](/code/functions/aiProviderHttpPost.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | POST with retry/backoff on 429 / 5xx / network errors. Ported from rfpAiHttpPostWithRetry so this file stands alone. |
| [aiProviderListOpenRouterModels()](/code/functions/aiProviderListOpenRouterModels.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | Fetch (and cache) the OpenRouter model catalogue. No API key required. Cached in system_settings as JSON for 24h to keep the model picker snappy. Falls back to a stale cache if a refresh fetch fails. |
| [aiProviderTraceStart()](/code/functions/aiProviderTraceStart.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | Record every HTTP exchange this file makes until `aiProviderTraceTake()`. |
| [aiProviderTraceTake()](/code/functions/aiProviderTraceTake.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | Stop recording and return what was recorded. |

| [aiSettingsForUi()](/code/functions/aiSettingsForUi.md) | [includes/ai_settings.php](/code/files/includes/ai_settings.php.md) | UI-safe view of the config — never returns the plaintext key, only a mask and a has_key flag. |
| [aiSettingsIsValidNs()](/code/functions/aiSettingsIsValidNs.md) | [includes/ai_settings.php](/code/files/includes/ai_settings.php.md) | True when `$ns` is a namespace registered in `aiSettingsRegistry()`. |

Defaults: provider/model from the registry; verify_ssl from the row, else the global SSL_VERIFY_PEER constant (preserves existing localhost behaviour). |
| [aiSettingsRegistry()](/code/functions/aiSettingsRegistry.md) | [includes/ai_settings.php](/code/files/includes/ai_settings.php.md) | Allowlisted AI config namespaces. Adding a module = one line here + dropping renderAiSettingsPanel('<ns>') on its settings page. The <ns>_api_key MUST also be added to ENCRYPTED_SETTING_KEYS + MASKED_SETTING_KEYS in encryption.php. |
| [aiSettingsSave()](/code/functions/aiSettingsSave.md) | [includes/ai_settings.php](/code/files/includes/ai_settings.php.md) | Persist a config. Validates provider; encrypts the key; honours masked/empty "no change" so saving provider+model alone never wipes the key. |
| [aiToolsResult()](/code/functions/aiToolsResult.md) | [includes/ai_provider.php](/code/files/includes/ai_provider.php.md) | Shared return shape for aiProviderChatTools. |
| [allAccessibleTenantsFilter()](/code/functions/allAccessibleTenantsFilter.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | The consolidated-view predicate: every company this analyst may see. The ONE copy of it, shared by `ticketTenantFilter()` and `activeTenantReadFilter()`. |
| [analystAnonymise()](/code/functions/analystAnonymise.md) | [includes/analyst_lifecycle.php](/code/files/includes/analyst_lifecycle.php.md) | Anonymise: everything Deactivate does, then the person behind the records is removed - personal settings and sign-in material deleted, the account's name and contact details replaced, and copies of the name next to their id replaced. The records themselves stay, credited to "Former analyst #N". Irreversible. |
| [analystCanAccessArticle()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessArticle.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst act on this article? Mirrors `analystCanAccessAsset()`, except a SHARED article (tenant_id IS NULL) belongs to everybody, so everybody may reach it — that is what "shared" means. |
| [analystCanAccessAsset()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessAsset.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst access this *asset* (by its owning company)? The Asset Management twin of `analystCanAccessChange()` — same rules (single-company → always true; NULL tenant treated as Default-owned; unknown id → false; part-migrated table → true). |
| [analystCanAccessChange()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessChange.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst access this *change* (by its owning company)? The Change Management twin of `analystCanAccessProblem()` — same rules (single-company → always true; NULL tenant treated as Default-owned; unknown id → false; part-migrated table → true). |
| [analystCanAccessChannel()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessChannel.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst administer this *messaging channel* (WhatsApp number, web chat widget) by its owning company? |
| [analystCanAccessCmdbObject()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessCmdbObject.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst access this *CMDB configuration item* (by its owning company)? The CMDB twin of `analystCanAccessAsset()` — same rules (single-company → always true; NULL tenant treated as Default-owned; unknown id → false; part-migrated table → true). |
| [analystCanAccessDomain()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessDomain.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst access this *domain* (#154)? The Domains-module twin of `analystCanAccessAsset()` below: single-company → always true; NULL tenant = the Default company; unknown id → false; part-migrated table → true. A domain is SCOPED DATA, never shared between companies. |
| [analystCanAccessModule()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessModule.md) | [includes/functions.php](/code/files/includes/functions.php.md) | Module access — the enforcement half of issue #30. Mirrors the admin gate above. True if `$analystId` may use `$moduleKey` under the current most/least policy. |
| [analystCanAccessProblem()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessProblem.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst access this *problem* (by its owning company)? The Problem Management twin of `analystCanAccessTicket()` — same rules (single-company → always true; NULL tenant treated as Default-owned; unknown id → false; part-migrated table → true). |
| [analystCanAccessProject()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessProject.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst access this *project* (by its company)? The same rules as a change: single-company -> yes; NULL company = the Default one; unknown id -> no. Added for documents on projects (3.3.0). |
| [analystCanAccessTask()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessTask.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst see/act on this task? (GH #83 groundwork.) |
| [analystCanAccessTenant()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessTenant.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst access this tenant? |
| [analystCanAccessTicket()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessTicket.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst access this *ticket* (by its owning company)? |
| [analystCanAccessUser()](/code/functions/analystCanAccessUser.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst act on this *end user / requester* (by their owning company)? |
| [analystCanAssignTenant()](/code/functions/analystCanAssignTenant.md) | [includes/tenancy.php](/code/files/includes/tenancy.php.md) | May this analyst PIN a channel (or anything else) to this company? |

| [analystDeactivate()](/code/functions/analystDeactivate.md) | [includes/analyst_lifecycle.php](/code/files/includes/analyst_lifecycle.php.md) | Deactivate: no sign-in, no access, API keys refused - and every name kept. Reversible by ticking Active again. Moving their open work is a separate, optional step (analystReassignLive()), done first by the screen. |
| [analystDelete()](/code/functions/analystDelete.md) | [includes/analyst_lifecycle.php](/code/files/includes/analyst_lifecycle.php.md) | Delete: only for an analyst nothing in history or setup refers to (once their open work has been moved). Personal settings and sign-in material go with them, in one transaction. Anything else refers to them -> refused, with the reason, so the screen can offer Deactivate or Anonymise instead. |

Authoritative DB check — use this for server-side enforcement (no session lag). |

Found while building the task twin of this endpoint (discussion #138): NOTHING here checked company - or even Tickets module access - so any signed-in analyst could read, attach to, tick and remove checklists on another company's ticket by number. The step and checklist actions are addressed by a CHILD id, so they resolve it to its ticket first and gate on that; a child id with no gate of its own is the classic way scoped data leaks. "Not found", never "not yours". |

Returns the (possibly updated) `$mailbox`. No-op for Google and app-only. |

Returns the lowercased email, or '' if it can't be determined. Used to back-fill authenticated_as for mailboxes that signed in before we started recording it. |
| [mailboxFetchAddresses()](/code/functions/mailboxFetchAddresses.md) | [includes/mailbox_graph.php](/code/files/includes/mailbox_graph.php.md) | Fetch ALL email addresses for the signed-in (delegated) mailbox — primary SMTP, UPN and every alias (proxyAddresses). This is how we tell that e.g. ed@ is just an alias of the edmozley@ mailbox: the token only carries the UPN, so we have to ask Graph for the alias list. Needs the lightweight User.Read scope; returns [] if it isn't granted (older mailboxes) so the caller can fall back to the token claim. |
| [mailboxGraphBase()](/code/functions/mailboxGraphBase.md) | [includes/mailbox_graph.php](/code/files/includes/mailbox_graph.php.md) | Per-request Graph base path: '/me' (delegated) or '/users/<addr>' (app-only). Set once after the mailbox is loaded; the Graph helpers read it back. |
| [mailboxGraphGet()](/code/functions/mailboxGraphGet.md) | [includes/mailbox_graph.php](/code/files/includes/mailbox_graph.php.md) | An authenticated Graph GET, shaped for `mailboxResolveFolderId()`. Every caller was writing the same twelve lines of curl. |
| [mailboxHealthProblems()](/code/functions/mailboxHealthProblems.md) | [includes/mailbox_health.php](/code/files/includes/mailbox_health.php.md) | Every warning here can be dismissed, because every one of them can legitimately be somebody's deliberate choice: a mailbox that genuinely should record no origin, a receive-only IMAP mailbox with no outgoing server, a low-traffic mailbox nobody minds being checked rarely. Dismissing says "I know", and the item drops out of the ! while staying visible, and reversible, inside it. |
| [mailboxIdentityFromToken()](/code/functions/mailboxIdentityFromToken.md) | [includes/mailbox_graph.php](/code/files/includes/mailbox_graph.php.md) | Pull the signed-in user's address straight out of a Graph access token. The token is a JWT whose payload carries the user's UPN / email — so we can read it offline, with NO Graph permission. This matters because the usual mailbox scopes (Mail.*) do NOT include User.Read, so a /me call 403s and can't tell us who signed in. Returns a lowercased email, or '' if the token has no usable claim. |
| [mailboxIdentityMismatch()](/code/functions/mailboxIdentityMismatch.md) | [includes/mailbox_graph.php](/code/files/includes/mailbox_graph.php.md) | Offline "is this reading the wrong inbox?" check for delegated Microsoft mailboxes. Returns an error message to show/throw, or null if it's fine. A configured target that matches ANY owned address (primary OR alias) passes; an unknown identity set (legacy mailboxes that predate alias capture) is grandfathered through. |
| [mailboxIdentityRecord()](/code/functions/mailboxIdentityRecord.md) | [includes/mailbox_graph.php](/code/files/includes/mailbox_graph.php.md) | Build the identity record for a freshly-authenticated delegated token: ['primary' => <display address>, 'addresses' => [every address this mailbox owns]]. Combines the token's own claim (always available, offline) with the Graph alias list (when User.Read is granted). 'primary' is what we show; 'addresses' is what we match the configured target against. |
| [mailboxIsAppOnly()](/code/functions/mailboxIsAppOnly.md) | [includes/mailbox_graph.php](/code/files/includes/mailbox_graph.php.md) | Is this a Microsoft mailbox authenticating as itself (client credentials)? There is no signed-in user in that mode, so '/me' is meaningless to Graph and every call has to name the mailbox: /users/<target_mailbox>. Google and IMAP mailboxes always behave as delegated. |

| [projectChatTools()](/code/functions/projectChatTools.md) | [includes/projects/assistant_chat.php](/code/files/includes/projects/assistant_chat.php.md) | The tools the model may call. Read tools run at once; propose_* tools only record a proposal. |
| [projectChatTurn()](/code/functions/projectChatTurn.md) | [includes/projects/assistant_chat.php](/code/files/includes/projects/assistant_chat.php.md) | One turn. `$mode`: 'chat' (the person wrote `$text`), 'open' (a greeting for an empty conversation) or 'resume' (a catch-up after a while). | [projectEarnedValue()](/code/functions/projectEarnedValue.md) | [includes/projects/budget.php](/code/files/includes/projects/budget.php.md) | Earned value (3.3.0), from what the budget already holds: BAC budget at completion - the planned total PV planned value by date - the cumulative planned spend (the timeline's planned line) EV earned value by date - BAC x the share of today's whole scope that was done by then: by estimated hours when the project's tasks have estimates, otherwise by count. It only ever rises. AC actual cost by date - the cumulative actual (the timeline's actual line) Today's indices: CPI = EV / AC (above 1 = under budget), SPI = EV / PV (above 1 = ahead), CV = EV - AC, SV = EV - PV, EAC = BAC / CPI, VAC = BAC - EAC. Null when there is no planned budget or no work to measure. |

An exception turns automatic health red. Worked out, never stored. |

| [sessionIsAdmin()](/code/functions/sessionIsAdmin.md) | [includes/functions.php](/code/files/includes/functions.php.md) | Cosmetic (UI-layer) admin check for pages: "should this analyst see System?". Reads `$_SESSION`['is_admin']; if absent (e.g. a session created before this upgrade) it self-heals by looking the flag up once and caching it in the session, so no login-path changes are needed and no admin is ever locked out. Requires a writable session (not read_and_close). For hard enforcement on APIs use `requireAdminJson()` instead, which re-checks the DB every call. |

| [warbotLike()](/code/functions/warbotLike.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | Escape the LIKE wildcards themselves, or a search for "50%" matches everything. |
| [warbotLooksLikeStatusNotice()](/code/functions/warbotLooksLikeStatusNotice.md) | [includes/warbot/warbot.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/warbot.php.md) | Is this one of Warbot's own "I am degraded" notices? |
| [warbotNoBrain()](/code/functions/warbotNoBrain.md) | [includes/warbot/warbot.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/warbot.php.md) | What Warbot says when the brain is unreachable but the hands still work. |
| [warbotPost()](/code/functions/warbotPost.md) | [includes/warbot/warbot.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/warbot.php.md) | Post Warbot's reply into a channel. |
| [warbotProjectAnswer()](/code/functions/warbotProjectAnswer.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | A ServiceError (no such project, several match) is an answer to give the room, not a failure. |
| [warbotProjectScope()](/code/functions/warbotProjectScope.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | The asking analyst's projects: their active company, or every company they can see under "All". |
| [warbotRunTool()](/code/functions/warbotRunTool.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | Run one tool. NEVER throws — a failure is returned as words, because "the CMDB lookup failed" is something the model can usefully tell the room, whereas an exception here would lose the whole answer. |
| [warbotSystemPrompt()](/code/functions/warbotSystemPrompt.md) | [includes/warbot/warbot.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/warbot.php.md) | The system prompt. |
| [warbotToolAllowed()](/code/functions/warbotToolAllowed.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | May this analyst use this tool? The capability, and - for a tool that names one (3.3.0, the Projects tools) - access to the module whose data it reads. The older tools name no module and are unchanged: whether the war room is install-wide is the open question in `warbotToolSearchKnowledge()` below. |
| [warbotToolAssetLookup()](/code/functions/warbotToolAssetLookup.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | asset_lookup: up to eight assets whose hostname, asset tag or service tag contains the query, with type, status, OS, location, last user and last seen. |
| [warbotToolImpactOf()](/code/functions/warbotToolImpactOf.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | impact_of: the first CMDB object whose name matches, and everything related to it one hop away in either direction, worded with the relationship's verb. Not the full blast radius - that walk stays in the CMDB module. |
| [warbotToolKnownErrors()](/code/functions/warbotToolKnownErrors.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | known_errors: up to six problem records matching the query in title, description, root cause or workaround - known errors first, workaround quoted. |
| [warbotToolListProjects()](/code/functions/warbotToolListProjects.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | list_projects: the projects the asker may see, ten by default, via `projectAssistList()`. |
| [warbotToolMorningChecks()](/code/functions/warbotToolMorningChecks.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | morning_checks: the morning check results for a date (today in UTC by default), listing the ones that are not clear, or the first few when all are. |
| [warbotToolOnCall()](/code/functions/warbotToolOnCall.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | on_call: who is on call on a date (today in UTC by default) and everyone rostered that day with their shift times, from the ticket rota. |
| [warbotToolOpenIncidents()](/code/functions/warbotToolOpenIncidents.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | open_incidents: the count of open tickets (optionally at one priority by name) and the newest few, by number, priority, status, subject and assignee. A confidential ticket's subject is withheld by `ticketAiSubjectSql()`. |
| [warbotToolProjectDates()](/code/functions/warbotToolProjectDates.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | project_dates: one project's key dates via `projectAssistDates()`, scoped to the projects the asker may see. |
| [warbotToolProjectOverview()](/code/functions/warbotToolProjectOverview.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | project_overview: one project's overview via `projectAssistOverview()`, scoped to the projects the asker may see. |
| [warbotToolProjectRaid()](/code/functions/warbotToolProjectRaid.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | project_raid: one project's RAID log via `projectAssistRaid()`, scoped to the projects the asker may see. |
| [warbotToolProjectTasks()](/code/functions/warbotToolProjectTasks.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | project_tasks: one project's tasks via `projectAssistTasks()`, scoped to the projects the asker may see. |
| [warbotToolRecentChanges()](/code/functions/warbotToolRecentChanges.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | recent_changes: up to 20 changes whose work or outage window started in the last N days (default 2, max 30), with state and owner. |
| [warbotToolRelatedTickets()](/code/functions/warbotToolRelatedTickets.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | related_tickets: the tickets linked to a ticket number, with the link type, direction and each one's status. |
| [warbotTools()](/code/functions/warbotTools.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | The tool registry: every read-only tool Warbot and the MCP server can call, keyed by name, each with the description the model reads, its JSON schema, the capability needed to run it (null = War Room access is enough) and the handler that answers it. |
| [warbotToolSearchChat()](/code/functions/warbotToolSearchChat.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | search_chat: up to eight War Room messages matching the query, through `warRoomSearch()` so only channels the asker can read are searched. |
| [warbotToolSearchKnowledge()](/code/functions/warbotToolSearchKnowledge.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | search_knowledge: up to eight live Knowledge article titles matching the query that the asking analyst may see - titles only, most viewed first. |
| [warbotToolServiceStatus()](/code/functions/warbotToolServiceStatus.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | service_status: the active Service Status services and up to ten open service-status incidents, with the services each affects and its latest note. |
| [warbotToolsFor()](/code/functions/warbotToolsFor.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | The tools this analyst may actually use, in the shape aiProviderChatTools wants. A capability they lack means the tool is not OFFERED at all, rather than offered and then refused — a model told about a tool it cannot use will keep reaching for it and narrate the failure to the room. |
| [warbotToolSupplierContact()](/code/functions/warbotToolSupplierContact.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | supplier_contact: up to three suppliers matching by name or contract title, each with its latest contracts (only in companies the analyst can reach) and its active contacts' phone numbers and email. |
| [warbotToolTicketSpike()](/code/functions/warbotToolTicketSpike.md) | [includes/warbot/tools.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/tools.php.md) | ticket_spike: tickets raised in the last N minutes against the average for the same time window over the previous seven days, calling three times the usual rate a spike, plus the newest subjects. |
| [warbotTryCommand()](/code/functions/warbotTryCommand.md) | [includes/warbot/warbot.php](/code/files/includes/warbot/warbot.php.md) | Run a slash command. Returns null if the text is not one. 🔑 NO MODEL INVOLVED — this is the path that still works during the outage. |
| [warRoomAddMembers()](/code/functions/warRoomAddMembers.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Add analysts to a channel. Idempotent — re-inviting somebody is not an error. |
| [warRoomAttachmentFor()](/code/functions/warRoomAttachmentFor.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | The row for an attachment the analyst is allowed to fetch, or null. |
| [warRoomCanAccessChannel()](/code/functions/warRoomCanAccessChannel.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | May this analyst read this channel? |
| [warRoomCanPostChannel()](/code/functions/warRoomCanPostChannel.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | May this analyst POST here? Read access, minus archived. |
| [warRoomChannel()](/code/functions/warRoomChannel.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | One channel row, or null. Raw — callers must still check access. |
| [warRoomChannelAudience()](/code/functions/warRoomChannelAudience.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Every analyst who can read this channel, as id => full name. |
| [warRoomChannelList()](/code/functions/warRoomChannelList.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Every channel this analyst can see, in display order, with unread counts. |
| [warRoomChannelName()](/code/functions/warRoomChannelName.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | The label for a channel row. |
| [warRoomCreateChannel()](/code/functions/warRoomCreateChannel.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Create a custom channel. Any analyst may — during an incident, needing an administrator to make you a room is exactly the wrong dependency. |
| [warroomD008Bytes()](/code/functions/warroomD008Bytes.md) | [api/system/debug-tools/D008_war_room.php](/code/files/api/system/debug-tools/D008_war_room.php.md) | Formats a byte count as B, KB or MB for the report. |
| [warRoomDeleteMessage()](/code/functions/warRoomDeleteMessage.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Delete a message: the author, or somebody with war_room.manage. |
| [warRoomDirectory()](/code/functions/warRoomDirectory.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Analysts this person could start a DM with. Active accounts only — offering to message somebody who left is a dead end you only discover after typing. |
| [warRoomEditMessage()](/code/functions/warRoomEditMessage.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Edit a message. The AUTHOR only — nobody else may put words in your mouth, not even an administrator, who can delete but not rewrite. |
| [warRoomEnsureChannels()](/code/functions/warRoomEnsureChannels.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Make sure the channels this analyst is entitled to actually exist as rows. |
| [warRoomIsMember()](/code/functions/warRoomIsMember.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | True when the analyst is a listed member of the channel - what decides access to a private channel or a DM. |
| [warRoomMarkRead()](/code/functions/warRoomMarkRead.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Remember how far this analyst has read, so the channel list can show what is new. Only ever moves forward: an out-of-order poll must not un-read a channel. |
| [warRoomMayManage()](/code/functions/warRoomMayManage.md) | [api/war-room/channels.php](/code/files/api/war-room/channels.php.md) | Who may reshape this channel: its creator, or someone with war_room.manage. |
| [warRoomMentionCounts()](/code/functions/warRoomMentionCounts.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Unread mentions per channel, so the channel list can mark a mention differently from ordinary unread. Being named is a different event from having missed something, and one badge for both would hide the one that needs you. |
| [warRoomMessageRow()](/code/functions/warRoomMessageRow.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | One message row, raw. Callers do their own access checks. |
| [warRoomMessages()](/code/functions/warRoomMessages.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Messages in a channel, oldest first, each with its attachments. |
| [warRoomMyMentions()](/code/functions/warRoomMyMentions.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | The notifications panel: messages naming this analyst that they have not read. |
| [warRoomOpenDm()](/code/functions/warRoomOpenDm.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Find or create the DM between two analysts. |
| [warRoomPresent()](/code/functions/warRoomPresent.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Who has polled recently, and whether they are in THIS channel or elsewhere in the war room. "Here" versus "around" is a genuinely useful distinction during an incident — it is the difference between nobody is reading this and everybody is in the other room. |
| [warRoomPrune()](/code/functions/warRoomPrune.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Apply the retention setting. |
| [warRoomResolveMentions()](/code/functions/warRoomResolveMentions.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Work out who a message names, and record it. |
| [warRoomRetentionDays()](/code/functions/warRoomRetentionDays.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Retention in days from settings. 0 (the default) means keep forever. |
| [warRoomSearch()](/code/functions/warRoomSearch.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Search the conversations this analyst can see. |
| [warRoomSend()](/code/functions/warRoomSend.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Post a message. Returns the new id, or 0 if the body was empty. |
| [warRoomSetArchived()](/code/functions/warRoomSetArchived.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Archive or restore a custom channel. Team and all-hands channels cannot be archived — they are derived, so the way to remove one is to remove the team. |
| [warRoomSitrepPrompt()](/code/functions/warRoomSitrepPrompt.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | The prompt for the situation report. |
| [warRoomSnippet()](/code/functions/warRoomSnippet.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | A window of the message around the first term that matched, so a result is readable without opening it. Plain text — the page inserts it with textContent and highlights client-side, so nothing here can emit markup. |
| [warRoomStoreAttachment()](/code/functions/warRoomStoreAttachment.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Store one uploaded file against a message. |
| [warRoomStoreMentions()](/code/functions/warRoomStoreMentions.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Record the mentions for a message. Idempotent via the UNIQUE on (message, analyst). |
| [warRoomSweepOrphanFiles()](/code/functions/warRoomSweepOrphanFiles.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Delete attachment files with no row left pointing at them. |
| [warRoomTouchPresence()](/code/functions/warRoomTouchPresence.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Record that this analyst is here, and return who else is. |
| [warRoomTranscriptSince()](/code/functions/warRoomTranscriptSince.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | The transcript to summarise: everything said in channels this analyst can see since a given moment. |
| [warRoomUpdateChannel()](/code/functions/warRoomUpdateChannel.md) | [includes/warroom.php](/code/files/includes/warroom.php.md) | Rename / re-topic a custom channel. Team and DM names are derived, never stored. |
| [webchatAddMessage()](/code/functions/webchatAddMessage.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Append a message to a conversation's pre-ticket transcript (webchat_messages). sender is 'visitor'\|'ai'\|'agent'\|'system'. `$sourceEmailId` links a mirrored analyst reply back to its `emails` row so it's only mirrored once. Returns the new id. |
| [webchatAiConfigured()](/code/functions/webchatAiConfigured.md) | [includes/webchat/ai.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/ai.php.md) | Is a Knowledge AI provider/key configured? (Cheap check before spending the budget.) |
| [webchatAiReply()](/code/functions/webchatAiReply.md) | [includes/webchat/ai.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/ai.php.md) | Draft an AI answer to `$question`, given the recent `$history` (array of ['sender','body']). Returns ['ok'=>bool, 'answer'=>string, 'articles'=>[{id,title}], 'error'=>?string]. |
| [webchatAiSummarise()](/code/functions/webchatAiSummarise.md) | [includes/webchat/ai.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/ai.php.md) | Summarise a web chat transcript into a short support-ticket description for a human agent — used as the ticket body on an "email me back" escalation. Returns '' when AI is unconfigured or the call fails, so the caller can fall back to the raw transcript. |
| [webchatBaseUrl()](/code/functions/webchatBaseUrl.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Public base URL + app root used to build widget asset / API URLs. Reuses the messaging public base (system setting or derived from the request) and derives the app root from SCRIPT_NAME so it works under any sub-path. Only ever called from a script under /api/webchat/, so that prefix is what we strip. |
| [webchatClientIp()](/code/functions/webchatClientIp.md) | [includes/webchat/public.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/public.php.md) | Best-effort client IP (rate limiting only — not a security boundary). |
| [webchatEmbedSnippet()](/code/functions/webchatEmbedSnippet.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | The <script> embed snippet a customer pastes just before </body> on their site. |
| [webchatFail()](/code/functions/webchatFail.md) | [includes/webchat/public.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/public.php.md) | Emit a JSON error and stop. |
| [webchatGenerateKey()](/code/functions/webchatGenerateKey.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | A fresh, URL-safe public widget key (e.g. wc_1a2b…). |
| [webchatGenerateToken()](/code/functions/webchatGenerateToken.md) | [includes/webchat/public.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/public.php.md) | A fresh per-conversation capability token (browser-held). |
| [webchatGetMessages()](/code/functions/webchatGetMessages.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Read a conversation's transcript messages after `$afterId` (ascending). |
| [webchatGetOrCreateUser()](/code/functions/webchatGetOrCreateUser.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Find-or-create the requester for a web chat conversation, keyed by the real email the visitor gave. Returns the user id, or null if the users table is unavailable. |
| [webchatHandlePreflight()](/code/functions/webchatHandlePreflight.md) | [includes/webchat/public.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/public.php.md) | Answer a CORS preflight and exit. Call at the very top of each public endpoint. |
| [webchatIngestMessage()](/code/functions/webchatIngestMessage.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Ingest one inbound visitor message for a web chat conversation. On the first message it opens a ticket and pins the conversation to it; afterwards it appends. Returns the ticket id. (Thin orchestration over webchatOpenTicket + webchatInsertInbound.) |
| [webchatInsertInbound()](/code/functions/webchatInsertInbound.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Store one inbound visitor message against a ticket in the shared `emails` table (channel='webchat'), so the reading-pane thread and reply path work unchanged. Returns the new email id. |
| [webchatInsertOutbound()](/code/functions/webchatInsertOutbound.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Store an automated/outbound web chat message against a ticket in `emails` (direction='Outbound', channel='webchat'), so the analyst sees it in the thread and the widget's reply-poll picks it up. Used to post the AI's answers alongside the ticket in assist mode. Returns the new email id. |
| [webchatIsOpenNow()](/code/functions/webchatIsOpenNow.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Is the widget "open" right now? A widget with no business-hours calendar (NULL) is always open. Otherwise the calendar's weekday hours + holidays are evaluated in the calendar's own timezone. A misconfigured calendar (missing, bad tz, no hours rows) fails open — availability should never hard-block a visitor from making contact. |
| [webchatLoadByChannel()](/code/functions/webchatLoadByChannel.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Load a widget row joined to its channel, keyed by channel id. Null if missing. |
| [webchatLoadByKey()](/code/functions/webchatLoadByKey.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Load a widget row joined to its channel, keyed by public widget key. Null if missing. |
| [webchatLoadConversation()](/code/functions/webchatLoadConversation.md) | [includes/webchat/public.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/public.php.md) | Load a conversation by its token, scoped to the widget's channel (so a token issued for one widget can't be replayed against another). Null if not found. |
| [webchatMirrorAgentReplies()](/code/functions/webchatMirrorAgentReplies.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Mirror any new analyst replies (Outbound webchat emails on the conversation's ticket) into the transcript, so an AI-enabled widget — which reads webchat_messages, not the emails table — shows them to the visitor. Deduped by source_email_id, so AI answers already recorded with their source email (and previously mirrored replies) are skipped. |
| [webchatOfflineMessage()](/code/functions/webchatOfflineMessage.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | The message shown to a visitor when the widget is closed (out of office hours). Uses the admin's custom offline message if set, else a sensible default — so a closed widget is never silent, even if no message was configured. |
| [webchatOpenTicket()](/code/functions/webchatOpenTicket.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Open a ticket for a web chat conversation and pin the conversation to it (one conversation == one ticket). Returns the new ticket id. Shared by the live-ingest path and the deflect-mode escalation path so ticket creation lives in one place. |
| [webchatOriginAllowed()](/code/functions/webchatOriginAllowed.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Is the request origin permitted for this widget? An empty allowlist means "any" (testing only). Matching is exact on scheme+host (+port), trailing slash ignored. |
| [webchatParseOrigins()](/code/functions/webchatParseOrigins.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Parse the stored allowed_origins blob into a clean list of origins. Accepts one per line (or comma-separated), trims trailing slashes, drops blanks/comments. |
| [webchatPromoteToTicket()](/code/functions/webchatPromoteToTicket.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | Promote a deflect-mode conversation to a real ticket. No-op returning the existing id if already ticketed. For `$mode` 'agent' the opening message is the full transcript; for 'email' the opening body is `$aiSummary` (or a fallback) and the full transcript is attached as a .txt. Returns the ticket id. |
| [webchatPublicHeaders()](/code/functions/webchatPublicHeaders.md) | [includes/webchat/public.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/public.php.md) | Emit CORS + JSON headers. Pass the request's origin to echo it back (required for the browser to accept the response on a cross-origin call); pass '*' when there is no origin (same-origin / curl). Call once the origin has been authorised. |
| [webchatPublicResolve()](/code/functions/webchatPublicResolve.md) | [includes/webchat/public.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/public.php.md) | Load the widget for a public request, enforce active + origin allowlist, and set the CORS headers. Returns the widget row (joined to its channel). Exits with JSON on any failure — so the caller can treat the return as authorised. |
| [webchatRateLimitSend()](/code/functions/webchatRateLimitSend.md) | [includes/webchat/public.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/public.php.md) | Cap inbound messages per conversation per minute. Exits (429) on breach. |
| [webchatRateLimitStart()](/code/functions/webchatRateLimitStart.md) | [includes/webchat/public.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/public.php.md) | Cap new conversations per IP per minute. Exits (429) on breach. |
| [webchatRequestOrigin()](/code/functions/webchatRequestOrigin.md) | [includes/webchat/public.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/public.php.md) | The origin of the requesting page. Cross-origin requests carry an Origin header (the authoritative case in production, where the customer's site and the FreeITSM host are different origins). Same-origin requests — e.g. a demo site on the same host — omit Origin entirely, so we fall back to the scheme+host of the Referer. Returns '' if neither is present. Like the whole allowlist, this is a deterrent, not a crypto control. |
| [webchatTranscriptText()](/code/functions/webchatTranscriptText.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | The full chat transcript as plain text — "Visitor: …" / "Assistant: …" / "Agent: …". Used as the opening message when a deflect conversation is escalated to a ticket, and as the .txt attachment on an email escalation. |
| [webchatVisitorName()](/code/functions/webchatVisitorName.md) | [includes/webchat/webchat.php](/code/files/includes/webchat/webchat.php.md) | The visitor's display name for a conversation (name → email → generic). |

Updates the row with the outcome and returns 'delivered' \| 'failed' \| 'dead'. |
| [webhookBackoffSeconds()](/code/functions/webhookBackoffSeconds.md) | [includes/webhook_delivery.php](/code/files/includes/webhook_delivery.php.md) | Seconds to wait before the next retry, keyed by the attempt that just failed. |
| [webhookDecrypt()](/code/functions/webhookDecrypt.md) | [includes/webhook_delivery.php](/code/files/includes/webhook_delivery.php.md) | Decrypt; plaintext passes straight through (pre-encryption rows still work). |

| [webhookEncryptionAvailable()](/code/functions/webhookEncryptionAvailable.md) | [includes/webhook_delivery.php](/code/files/includes/webhook_delivery.php.md) | True when an encryption key is actually usable — drives the honesty banner. |

Returns the new delivery id. |

Shared by the delivery worker and the editor's "Send test" preview so both use identical transport (method, timeouts, TLS verification) — the test can never behave differently from a real delivery. |
| [webhookPayloadRetentionDays()](/code/functions/webhookPayloadRetentionDays.md) | [includes/webhook_delivery.php](/code/files/includes/webhook_delivery.php.md) | Days to keep payload bodies. 0 = never store, -1 = forever. |
| [webhookPurgePayloads()](/code/functions/webhookPurgePayloads.md) | [includes/webhook_delivery.php](/code/files/includes/webhook_delivery.php.md) | Blank the stored request/response bodies of deliveries past the retention window. Returns how many rows were scrubbed. Called by the cron worker after each queue run, so retention is enforced without another scheduled task. |
| [webhookRedactUrl()](/code/functions/webhookRedactUrl.md) | [includes/webhook_delivery.php](/code/files/includes/webhook_delivery.php.md) | A URL safe to show in the delivery log: keeps the bit that identifies WHERE it went, hides the bit that lets you POST there. A Discord webhook URL ends in a token; printing it in a log everyone can read defeats encrypting it. |
| [webhookReplay()](/code/functions/webhookReplay.md) | [includes/webhook_delivery.php](/code/files/includes/webhook_delivery.php.md) | Reset a delivery so the worker sends it again (the "Replay" button). |
| [webhookReplayBlockedReason()](/code/functions/webhookReplayBlockedReason.md) | [includes/webhook_delivery.php](/code/files/includes/webhook_delivery.php.md) | Why a replay was refused — so the UI can say "the payload was purged by your retention setting" rather than a generic failure. Replay re-sends the STORED body, so a purged row has nothing to send and must not be re-queued (that would POST an empty payload to a live endpoint). |
| [webhookRunQueue()](/code/functions/webhookRunQueue.md) | [includes/webhook_delivery.php](/code/files/includes/webhook_delivery.php.md) | Deliver up to `$limit` due rows. Claims each atomically (so overlapping worker runs never double-send). Returns counts. |

A no-op on every other retention setting. |

| [wf_emit()](/code/functions/wf_emit.md) | [includes/functions.php](/code/files/includes/functions.php.md) | Fire a settings/CRUD workflow event ({entity}.{created\|updated\|deleted}) from a UI settings endpoint, without each file having to require the workflow engine. Lazily loads the engine and is fully self-safe — swallows any Throwable (including a missing engine on a minimal install) so it can NEVER affect the save it follows. Funnels into the exact same WorkflowEngine::dispatch() path as every other event, so a webhook fires identically however it was emitted. |

A contract expiring in 45 days crosses the 90-day window today and the 30-day window in a fortnight — two separate emissions, each carrying `window_days`, so a workflow can say "only tell me at 30 days" with a plain condition rather than needing its own scheduling. |
